) AS [w1]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [w0].[Id], [w0].[AddedAt], [w0].[ProductId], [w0].[WishlistId], [p].[Id] AS [Id0], [p].[Brand], [p].[Category], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[ImageUrl], [p].[IsActive], [p].[IsFeatured], [p].[OriginalPrice], [p].[Price], [p].[Rating], [p].[ReviewCount], [p].[StockQuantity], [p].[Title], [p].[UpdatedAt]

) AS [s] ON [w1].[Id] = [s].[WishlistId]

    FROM [Wishlists] AS [w]
    WHERE [w].[UserId] = @userId
) AS [w1]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [w0].[Id], [w0].[AddedAt], [w0].[ProductId], [w0].[WishlistId], [p].[Id] AS [Id0], [p].[Brand], [p].[Category], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[Description], [p].[ImageUrl], [p].[IsActive], [p].[IsFeatured], [p].[OriginalPrice], [p].[Price], [p].[Rating], [p].[ReviewCount], [p].[StockQuantity], [p].[Title], [p].[UpdatedAt]
